---
title: LUA Enums
description: The LUA Enums for grandMA3 version v2.1
version: v2.1

import { Aside } from '@astrojs/starlight/components';

<Aside type="tip">
  The following enums can be access from the global `Enums` object available
  in LUA.

  ```lua
  Printf(Enums.AgendaMode.Sunset)
  ```
  Also shown in the **Usage** column for each value
</Aside>

| Name | Value | Usage
| ---- | ----- | -----
| `The responder cannot comply with request because the message is not implemented in responder.` | 0 | `Enums.RDMResponseNackReason.The responder cannot comply with request because the message is not implemented in responder.`
| `The responder cannot interpret request as controller data was not formatted correctly.` | 1 | `Enums.RDMResponseNackReason.The responder cannot interpret request as controller data was not formatted correctly.`
| `The responder cannot comply due to an internal hardware fault.` | 2 | `Enums.RDMResponseNackReason.The responder cannot comply due to an internal hardware fault.`
| `Proxy is not the RDM line master and cannot comply with message.` | 3 | `Enums.RDMResponseNackReason.Proxy is not the RDM line master and cannot comply with message.`
| `SET Command normally allowed but being blocked currently.` | 4 | `Enums.RDMResponseNackReason.SET Command normally allowed but being blocked currently.`
| `Not valid for Command Class attempted. May be used where GET allowed but SET is not supported.` | 5 | `Enums.RDMResponseNackReason.Not valid for Command Class attempted. May be used where GET allowed but SET is not supported.`
| `Value for given Parameter out of allowable range or not supported.` | 6 | `Enums.RDMResponseNackReason.Value for given Parameter out of allowable range or not supported.`
| `Buffer or Queue space currently has no free space to store data.` | 7 | `Enums.RDMResponseNackReason.Buffer or Queue space currently has no free space to store data.`
| `Incoming message exceeds buffer capacity.` | 8 | `Enums.RDMResponseNackReason.Incoming message exceeds buffer capacity.`
| `Sub-Device is out of range or unknown.` | 9 | `Enums.RDMResponseNackReason.Sub-Device is out of range or unknown.`
| `The proxy buffer is full and can not store any more Queued Message or Status Message responses.` | 10 | `Enums.RDMResponseNackReason.The proxy buffer is full and can not store any more Queued Message or Status Message responses.`
